To investigate and fix a crash that occurs when the Android app is left idle in the background for a few minutes, the files most likely to need changes are related to app lifecycle management, background services, or state restoration on Android.

When an Android app crashes after being left in the background for a few minutes, it is very frequently caused by **background tasks (like WorkManager)** executing in a separate background isolate where dependencies (such as GetIt, database instances, or platform bindings) have **not been initialized**. 

When Workmanager triggers a periodic task in the background, it spins up a new Dart isolate that does not run your `main()` function. Therefore, if `callbackDispatcher` tries to access `getIt<AppDatabase>()` or `getIt<DocsUseCase>()` without initializing `WidgetsFlutterBinding` and setting up your dependency injection container first, it throws an unhandled exception and crashes.

To fix this, please add the following file to the chat:
- `lib/core/utils/background/workmanager_initializer.dart`

> Tokens: 5.0k sent, 163 received. Cost: $0.0019 message, $0.0051 session.  
> lib/core/utils/background/workmanager_initializer.dart  
> Add file to the chat? (Y)es/(N)o/(D)on't ask again [Yes]: y  

1. Determine if any code changes are needed.
Yes. When WorkManager runs a background task, it spawns a separate background isolate where Flutter bindings are not automatically initialized, and dependency injection (GetIt) has not been set up. Calling `startUploadDocs()` without initializing `WidgetsFlutterBinding` and DI causes an unhandled exception/crash when the app is in the background.

2. Explain any needed changes.
We need to add `WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();` inside the `callbackDispatcher` function so that platform channels and bindings are ready before any background work or database/network operations are executed.

3. Output the updated file content.

lib/core/utils/background/workmanager_initializer.dart
```
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:developer';
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:workmanager/workmanager.dart';
import 'background_tasks.dart';

const String uploadTaskName = 'upload_docs_task';

@pragma('vm:entry-point')
void callbackDispatcher() {
  Workmanager().executeTask((taskName, inputData) async {
    WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
    
    if (taskName == uploadTaskName) {
      await startUploadDocs();
    }
    return Future.value(true);
  });
}

@singleton
class WorkManagerInitializer {
  Future<void> initialize() async {
    await Workmanager().initialize(callbackDispatcher, isInDebugMode: true);
  }

  void startPendingUploads() {
    startUploadDocs().catchError((error) {
      log('خطأ في بدء رفع التوثيقات المعلقة: $error');
    });
  }

  Future<void> registerSystemUploadTask() async {
    await Workmanager().registerPeriodicTask(
      'unique_upload_docs_task',
      uploadTaskName,
      frequency: const Duration(minutes: 15),
      initialDelay: const Duration(minutes: 15),
      constraints: Constraints(networkType: NetworkType.connected),
      foregroundServiceConfig: ForegroundServiceConfig(
        notificationTitle: 'رفع التوثيقات المعلقة',
        notificationText: 'جاري رفع التوثيقات في الخلفية...',
        notificationChannelId: 'sync_docs_channel',
        notificationChannelName: 'رفع التوثيقات المعلقة',
        notificationId: 24,
        foregroundServiceType: ForegroundServiceType.dataSync,
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

1. Determine if any code changes are needed.
Yes. In `background_tasks.dart`, `startUploadDocs()` attempts to use `getIt<AppDatabase>()` and other dependencies. Because WorkManager runs tasks in a separate background isolate, the `GetIt` container is completely empty there unless initialized. This is the primary reason for background crashes.

2. Explain any needed changes.
We need to check if `GetIt` has been initialized in the current background isolate (e.g., checking if `AppDatabase` is registered), and if not, call `configureDependencies()` before proceeding with any database or use case calls.

3. Output the updated file content.

lib/core/utils/background/background_tasks.dart
```
import 'dart:developer';
import 'package:error_handler/error_handler.dart';

import '../../../features/orders_features/cached_docs/data/datasources/local_data_src/drift/app_database.dart';
import '../../../features/orders_features/cached_docs/data/datasources/local_data_src/drift/tables/items_table.dart';
import '../../../features/orders_features/cached_docs/domain/usecases/cached_docs_use_cases.dart';
import '../../../features/orders_features/docs/data/mapper/docs_mapper.dart';
import '../../../features/orders_features/docs/domain/entities/doc_entity.dart';
import '../../../features/orders_features/docs/domain/usecases/docs_use_cases.dart';
import '../../di/dependency_injection.dart';

Future<void> startUploadDocs() async {
  try {
    if (!getIt.isRegistered<AppDatabase>()) {
      await configureDependencies();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    log('Error configuring dependencies in background isolate: $e');
  }

  final db = getIt<AppDatabase>();
  final docsUseCase = getIt<DocsUseCase>();
  await getIt<CachedDocsUseCases>().getPendings().then((res) async {
    await res.when(
      success: (docs) async {
        for (final DocEntity doc in docs ?? []) {
          try {
            await docsUseCase.updateDoc(docReq: doc.toUpdateDocReq());
          } on Exception catch (e) {
            await db.docsDao.updateUploadStatus(
              id: doc.id,
              status: UploadStatus.failed.name,
            );
          }
        }
      },
      failure: (error) {
        log('error: $error');
      },
    );
  });
}
```

To ensure `lib/features/home_features` strictly complies with the project's architecture, styling, and file-length rules, please follow these instructions:

### 1. Audit File Lengths (The 100-Line Limit Rule)
- Inspect all files under `lib/features/home_features/` (including views, blocs, repositories, and models).
- If any file exceeds 100 lines of code, refactor and split it into smaller, cohesive widgets or helper files.

### 2. Mandatory Theme & Responsive Styling (`flutter_style.md`)
- Replace any hardcoded colors (e.g., `Color(0xFF...)`, `Colors.green`) with theme-driven colors: `Theme.of(context).colorScheme...`.
- Replace explicit text styles with `Theme.of(context).textTheme...`.
- Ensure all padding, margins, and border radii combine dot shorthand with `ScreenUtil` extensions (`.r`, `.w`, `.h`, `.sp`), e.g., `.all(16.r)`, `.circular(12.r)`. Avoid raw numbers like `.all(16)` or `EdgeInsets.all(...)`.

### 3. Iconography Rules (`components.md`)
- Ensure all UI icons strictly come from `Fontawesome` or `lucide_icons_flutter` libraries. Avoid standard Material `Icons.xxx` wherever possible.

### 4. Clean Architecture & Modularization
- Confirm strict separation between `data/`, `domain/`, and `presentation/` (or `present/views/`).
- If any folder contains more than 4 files or mixed responsibilities, modularize them into dedicated sub-directories (`widgets/`, `blocs/`, `models/`, etc.) with proper barrel exports.

To ensure the `lib/features/notifications` feature fully complies with Clean Architecture, the 100-line file limit, ScreenUtil extensions, and theme-driven styling, follow these instructions for inspection and refactoring:

### 1. File Length & Modularity Audit
- Inspect all files under `lib/features/notifications/` (including data sources, repositories, use cases, BLoCs, and views/widgets).
- If any file exceeds **100 lines of code**, split it into smaller, cohesive widgets or helper classes as mandated by `file_limits.md`.

### 2. Presentation & UI Refactoring (`presentation` / `present`)
- **Theme & Colors:** Ensure **ZERO** hardcoded colors (like `Colors.white`, `Color(0xFF...)`) or raw `TextStyle` instantiations. Pull all colors from `Theme.of(context).colorScheme` and typography from `Theme.of(context).textTheme`.
- **ScreenUtil Extensions:** Ensure all padding, margins, spacing, and border radii combine dot shorthand with responsive extensions (`.r`, `.w`, `.h`, `.sp`), e.g., `padding: .all(16.r)`, `borderRadius: .circular(8.r)`.
- **Iconography:** Replace any Material `Icons.xxx` with icons from `lucide_icons_flutter` or `font_awesome_flutter`.

### 3. Folder Structure Verification
- Verify the structure strictly adheres to Clean Architecture:
  - `lib/features/notifications/data/` (models, datasources, repository implementations)
  - `lib/features/notifications/domain/` (entities, repository contracts, use cases)
  - `lib/features/notifications/presentation/` or `present/` (blocs, states, events, views, and widgets)

```dart
import 'package:dartz/dartz.dart';
```

```dart
class GetS3ItemsUseCase {
```

lib/features/s3/data/models/s3_item_model.dart
```dart
import '../../domain/entities/s3_item_entity.dart';

class S3ItemModel extends S3ItemEntity {
  const S3ItemModel({
    required String id,
    required String name,
    required String url,
  }) : super(id: id, name: name, url: url);

  factory S3ItemModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return S3ItemModel(
      id: json['id'] as String,
      name: json['name'] as String,
      url: json['url'] as String,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'url': url,
    };
  }
}
```

lib/features/s3/data/repositories/s3_repository_impl.dart
```dart
import 'package:dartz/dartz.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/s3_item_entity.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/s3_repository.dart';
import '../datasources/s3_remote_data_source.dart';

class S3RepositoryImpl implements S3Repository {
  final S3RemoteDataSource remoteDataSource;

  const S3RepositoryImpl(this.remoteDataSource);

  @override
  Future<Either<Exception, List<S3ItemEntity>>> getItems() async {
    try {
      final result = await remoteDataSource.fetchItems();
      return Right(result);
    } catch (e) {
      return Left(Exception(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}
```

lib/features/s3/presentation/bloc/s3_bloc.dart
```dart
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import '../../domain/usecases/get_s3_items_usecase.dart';
import 's3_event.dart';
import 's3_state.dart';

class S3Bloc extends Bloc<S3Event, S3State> {
  final GetS3ItemsUseCase getS3ItemsUseCase;

  S3Bloc(this.getS3ItemsUseCase) : super(const S3Initial()) {
    on<FetchS3ItemsEvent>(_onFetchS3Items);
  }

  Future<void> _onFetchS3Items(
    FetchS3ItemsEvent event,
    Emitter<S3State> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const S3Loading());
    final result = await getS3ItemsUseCase();
    result.fold(
      (failure) => emit(const S3Error('Failed to fetch items')),
      (items) => emit(S3Loaded(items)),
    );
  }
}
```

Here is the architectural direction for reviewing and refactoring the `lib/features/auth` feature according to the project's rules and engineering standards:

### 1. File Length Enforcement (< 100 Lines)
- Inspect all files under `lib/features/auth/` (including sub-features like `sign_in`, `sign_up`, `forgot_password`, `change_pass`, and `auth`).
- Identify any file exceeding **100 lines of code**.
- Proactively split large UI files into smaller, focused widgets under a `presentation/views/widgets/` sub-folder, or extract logic/helpers into dedicated utility files.

### 2. Zero Hardcoded Styles & Responsive Design (`ScreenUtil`)
- **Colors & Typography:** Eliminate any hardcoded `Color(0xFF...)`, `Colors.green`, `Colors.white`, or standalone `TextStyle(...)` declarations.
- **Theme Integration:** Strictly pull all colors from `context.colorScheme` (or `Theme.of(context).colorScheme`) and text styles from `context.textTheme`.
- **Responsive Extensions:** Ensure every spacing, padding, margin, `EdgeInsets`, `BorderRadius`, and font size combines dot shorthand with ScreenUtil extensions (`.r`, `.w`, `.h`, `.sp`), e.g., `padding: .all(16.r)`, `borderRadius: .circular(12.r)`.

### 3. Components & Forms Rules
- **Phone Fields:** Ensure all phone number inputs use `CustomPhoneFormField` located at `@lib/core/widgets/forms/custom_phone_form_field.dart`.
- **Iconography:** Replace standard Material `Icons.xxx` with icons from `Fontawesome` or `lucide_icons_flutter` libraries across all UI components in the auth feature.

### 4. Clean Architecture Directory Validation
Verify that sub-features under `lib/features/auth/` maintain clear separation:
- `data/`: Models, remote/local data sources, repository implementations.
- `domain/`: Entities, repository interfaces (abstract contracts), and use cases.
- `presentation/` or `present/`: State management (Bloc/Cubit) and view screens.

To ensure the feature under `lib/features/home_features` fully complies with the project's architecture, file size limits (< 100 lines per file), and styling rules, please follow these instructions:

### 1. Clean Architecture & Folder Modularization Check
Verify that `lib/features/home_features/` follows the strict Clean Architecture structure:
- `data/`: Models, remote data sources, repository implementations.
- `domain/`: Entities, repository interfaces, use cases.
- `presentation/` (or `present/`): Blocs/Cubit and View files.

### 2. File Length Limit Enforcement (< 100 Lines)
- Inspect every Dart file in `lib/features/home_features/`.
- If any file (especially screens, widgets, or blocs) exceeds **100 lines of code**, immediately refactor and split it into smaller, cohesive widgets under a `widgets/` subfolder (e.g., `lib/features/home_features/home/present/views/widgets/`).
- Create proper barrel export files if necessary.

### 3. Zero Hardcoded Styles & Colors
- Search through all UI files in `lib/features/home_features/` for any hardcoded colors (e.g., `Color(0xFF...)`, `Colors.grey`) or explicit `TextStyle` instantiations.
- Replace them with theme extensions:
  - Colors: `Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary` or `context.colorScheme...`
  - Typography: `Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium` or `context.textTheme...`
- Ensure **all** padding, margins, spacing, and border radii use ScreenUtil extensions (`.r`, `.w`, `.h`, `.sp`) combined with dot shorthand (e.g., `padding: .all(16.r)`, `borderRadius: .circular(12.r)`). Avoid static `EdgeInsets.` or `BorderRadius.` calls.

### 4. Iconography Rules
- Replace any standard Material icons (`Icons.xxx`) with icons from `lucide_icons_flutter` or `FontAwesome` libraries across all views in this feature.

To refactor and improve the `notifications` feature located at `lib/features/notifications` according to Clean Architecture, strict file-length limits (< 100 lines), theme-driven styling via `ScreenUtil` (`.r`, `.w`, `.h`, `.sp`), and proper widget modularization, here are the architectural instructions for the editor engineer.

### 1. Reorganize Folder Structure
Ensure the feature adheres strictly to Clean Architecture and the 4-file modularization rule by organizing into sub-directories:
```
lib/features/notifications/
├── data/
│   ├── datasources/
│   │   └── notifications_api.dart
│   ├── models/
│   │   └── notification_model.dart
│   └── repo_impl/
│       └── notifications_repo_impl.dart
├── domain/
│   ├── entities/
│   │   └── notification_entity.dart
│   ├── repo/
│   │   └── notifications_repo.dart
│   └── usecases/
│       └── notifications_use_cases.dart
└── presentation/
    ├── bloc/
    │   ├── notifications_bloc.dart
    │   ├── notifications_event.dart
    │   └── notifications_state.dart
    └── views/
        ├── notifications_view.dart
        └── widgets/
            ├── notification_item_widget.dart
            └── notification_list_widget.dart
```

---

### 2. File Refactoring Guidelines (Enforcing < 100 Lines & Theme-Driven Styling)

#### A. View: `lib/features/notifications/presentation/views/notifications_view.dart`
- Must be a stateless or stateful widget orchestrating the BLoC.

```dart
class NotificationItemWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final NotificationEntity notification;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const NotificationItemWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.notification,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: .symmetric(vertical: 6.h),
      padding: .all(12.r),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest,
        borderRadius: .circular(12.r),
      ),
      child: ListTile(
        onTap: onTap,
        title: Text(
          notification.title ?? '',
          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
        ),
        subtitle: Text(
          notification.body ?? '',
          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

#### C. Widget: `lib/features/notifications/presentation/views/widgets/notification_list_widget.dart`
- Handle BlocBuilder states (`loading`, `loaded`, `failure`) cleanly in under 100 lines.

```dart
class NotificationListWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  const NotificationListWidget({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocBuilder<NotificationsBloc, NotificationsState>(
      builder: (context, state) {
        return state.when(
          initial: () => const SizedBox.shrink(),
          loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
          loaded: (notifications) {
            if (notifications.isEmpty) {
              return Center(
                child: Text(
                  'No notifications found',
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
                ),
              );
            }
            return ListView.builder(
              itemCount: notifications.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                return NotificationItemWidget(
                  notification: notifications[index],
                  onTap: () {
                    // Handle tap
                  },
                );
              },
            );
          },
          failure: (error) => Center(
            child: Text(
              error.message ?? 'Error loading notifications',
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                    color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
                  ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_screenutil/flutter_screenutil.dart';
import 'package:gap/gap.dart';

import '../../../../core/theme/core/extensions/theme_ext.dart';
import '../../../domain/entities/notification_entity.dart';

class NotificationItemWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final NotificationEntity item;

  const NotificationItemWidget({super.key, required this.item});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: .all(12.r),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colorScheme.surface,
        borderRadius: .circular(8.r),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: context.colorScheme.shadow.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 4.r,
            offset: Offset(0, 2.h),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: .start,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: .all(8.r),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: context.colorScheme.primary.withOpacity(0.1),
              borderRadius: .circular(8.r),
            ),
            child: Icon(
              Icons.notifications,
              size: 20.sp,
              color: context.colorScheme.primary,
            ),
          ),
          Gap(12.w),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: .start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  item.title,
                  style: context.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
                Gap(4.h),
                Text(
                  item.body,
                  style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                    color: context.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                  ),
                ),
                Gap(8.h),
                Text(
                  item.createdAt,
                  style: context.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                    color: context.colorScheme.outline,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

lib/features/notifications/presentation/views/widgets/notification_list_widget.dart
```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:flutter_screenutil/flutter_screenutil.dart';
import 'package:gap/gap.dart';

import '../../../../core/theme/core/extensions/theme_ext.dart';
import '../../../../core/widgets/custom_empty_widget.dart';
import '../../bloc/notifications_bloc.dart';
import 'notification_item_widget.dart';

class NotificationListWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  const NotificationListWidget({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocBuilder<NotificationsBloc, NotificationsState>(
      builder: (context, state) {
        return state.maybeWhen(
          loading: () => Center(
            child: CircularProgressIndicator(
              color: context.colorScheme.primary,
            ),
          ),
          loaded: (notifications) {
            if (notifications.isEmpty) {
              return CustomEmptyWidget(
                icon: Icon(
                  Icons.notifications_off,
                  size: 40.sp,
                  color: context.colorScheme.primary,
                ),
                title: 'لا توجد إشعارات',
                subtitle:
                    'لا توجد إشعارات متاحة حاليًا، سيتم عرض الإشعارات هنا عند توفرها',
              );
            }
            return ListView.separated(
              padding: .all(16.r),
              itemCount: notifications.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                final noti = notifications[i];
                return NotificationItemWidget(item: noti);
              },
              separatorBuilder: (context, i) {
                return Gap(10.h);
              },
            );
          },
          failure: (error) => Center(
            child: Text(
              'حدث خطأ ما',
              style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                color: context.colorScheme.error,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          orElse: () => const SizedBox.shrink(),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_screenutil/flutter_screenutil.dart';

class NotificationsView extends StatefulWidget {
  const NotificationsView({super.key});

  static const String routeName = 'notifications';

  @override
  State<NotificationsView> createState() => _NotificationsViewState();
}

class _NotificationsViewState extends State<NotificationsView> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    getIt<NotificationsBloc>().add(const NotificationsEvent.getNotifications());
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: context.colorScheme.surface,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(
          'الإشعارات',
          style: context.textTheme.titleLarge,
        ),
        backgroundColor: context.colorScheme.surface,
        elevation: 0,
      ),
      body: Container(
        width: .infinity,
        height: .infinity,
        color: context.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest.withOpacity(0.3),
        child: const NotificationListWidget(),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:flutter_screenutil/flutter_screenutil.dart';
import 'package:gap/gap.dart';

import '../../../../core/theme/core/extensions/theme_ext.dart';
import '../../../../core/widgets/custom_empty_widget.dart';
import '../../bloc/notifications_bloc.dart';
import 'notification_item_widget.dart';

class NotificationListWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  const NotificationListWidget({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocBuilder<NotificationsBloc, NotificationsState>(
      builder: (context, state) {
        return state.maybeWhen(
          loading: () => Center(
            child: CircularProgressIndicator(
              color: context.colorScheme.primary,
            ),
          ),
          loaded: (notifications) {
            if (notifications.isEmpty) {
              return CustomEmptyWidget(
                icon: Icon(
                  Icons.notifications_off,
                  size: 40.sp,
                  color: context.colorScheme.primary,
                ),
                title: 'لا توجد إشعارات',
                subtitle:
                    'لا توجد إشعارات متاحة حاليًا، سيتم عرض الإشعارات هنا عند توفرها',
              );
            }
            return ListView.separated(
              padding: EdgeInsets.all(16.r),
              itemCount: notifications.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                final noti = notifications[i];
                return NotificationItemWidget(item: noti);
              },
              separatorBuilder: (context, i) {
                return Gap(10.h);
              },
            );
          },
          failure: (error) => Center(
            child: Text(
              'حدث خطأ ما',
              style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                color: context.colorScheme.error,
              ),
            ),
          ),
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'widgets/notification_list_widget.dart';

class NotificationsView extends StatefulWidget {
  const NotificationsView({super.key});

  static const String routeName = 'notifications';

  @override
  State<NotificationsView> createState() => _NotificationsViewState();
}

class _NotificationsViewState extends State<NotificationsView> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    getIt<NotificationsBloc>().add(const NotificationsEvent.getNotifications());
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: context.colorScheme.surface,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(
          'الإشعارات',
          style: context.textTheme.titleLarge,
        ),
        backgroundColor: context.colorScheme.surface,
        elevation: 0,
      ),
      body: Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        height: double.infinity,
        color: context.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest.withOpacity(0.3),
        child: const NotificationListWidget(),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### 3. Flutter Style & Theme Compliance (`flutter_style.md`)

- **Block Bodies**: Use full block bodies `{ return ...; }` for methods and widget builders instead of arrow syntax (`=>`).

To inspect and improve the feature in `lib/features/orders_features` to fully match the requested standards and rules, here are the step-by-step instructions for your editor engineer:

### 1. Audit & Restructure File Lengths (< 100 lines)
- Check all files under `lib/features/orders_features/` (including subfolders like `cached_docs`, `docs`, `orders`, `salla_orders_items`).
- Any file exceeding 100 lines must be split into smaller, cohesive widgets or helper classes. For instance, large widget builders in PDF previews or order lists should be extracted into dedicated stateless widget components under `presentation/views/widgets/`.

### 2. Enforce Strict Theme-Driven Styling & ScreenUtil Extensions
- Remove any explicit colors like `Color(0xFF...)`, `Colors.grey`, `Colors.blue`, etc.
- Replace them with theme colors: `Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary`, `surface`, `error`, etc., or use the `context.colorScheme` / `context.textTheme` extensions.
- Ensure all paddings, margins, border radii, and sizes use ScreenUtil extensions correctly with dot shorthand:
  - DO: `padding: .all(16.r)`
  - DO: `borderRadius: .circular(12.r)`
  - DO: `SizedBox(height: 12.h, width: 8.w)`
  - DO: `fontSize: 14.sp`

### 3. Iconography Standardization
- Replace any standard Material `Icons.xxx` usages with `FontAwesome` or `lucide_icons_flutter` icons across all widgets in `orders_features`.

### 4. Clean Architecture Compliance & Folder Structure Check
Ensure every sub-feature under `orders_features` strictly adheres to:
- `data/`: models, datasources, repo implementations.
- `domain/`: entities, repository contracts, use cases.
- `presentation/`: blocs/cubits and state files.
- `present/views/`: screens and UI components.

To inspect and refactor the `lib/features/splash` feature to strictly adhere to the Clean Architecture directory structure, Flutter style rules, file length limits (< 100 lines), and dynamic theme-driven styling, here are the instructions for your editor engineer:

### 1. Target Directory Structure
Ensure the `lib/features/splash/` folder is organized into the following Clean Architecture layout:
```text
lib/features/splash/
├── data/
│   └── datasources/ (if any local/remote sources are needed)
├── domain/
│   ├── entities/
│   ├── repo/
│   └── usecases/
└── present/
    ├── bloc/
    │   ├── splash_bloc.dart
    │   ├── splash_event.dart
    │   └── splash_state.dart
    └── views/
        └── splash_view.dart
```

### 2. Refactoring Guidelines & Rules
- **File Length Limit:** Keep every file under **100 lines of code**. If `splash_view.dart` or any other file exceeds this, extract sub-widgets into `lib/features/splash/present/views/widgets/`.
- **Theme-Driven Styling & Zero Hardcoded Colors:** 
  - Never use `Color(0xFF...)` or `Colors.white` / `Colors.black`.
  - Always use `Theme.of(context).colorScheme` and `Theme.of(context).textTheme`.
- **Responsive Spacing (ScreenUtil):**
  - Always combine dot shorthands and sizes with `.r`, `.w`, `.h`, or `.sp` (e.g., `padding: .all(16.r)`, `SizedBox(height: 24.h)`).
- **Block Bodies:** Always use full block bodies `{ return ...; }` for methods and widget builders instead of arrow syntax (`=>`).

### 3. Implementation Steps for the Editor Engineer
1. **Domain Layer (`lib/features/splash/domain/`)**: Ensure lightweight entities and use cases handle any initialization checks (e.g., checking authentication state or app configuration) and return `Future<ExecuteGuard<T>>` when dealing with operations that can fail.
2. **Presentation BLoC (`lib/features/splash/present/bloc/`)**: Implement `SplashBloc`, `SplashEvent`, and `SplashState` using Freezed or standard BLoC patterns, handling loading and navigation triggers.
3. **View Layer (`lib/features/splash/present/views/splash_view.dart`)**: 
   - Use `CustomScaffold`.
   - Pull background colors or text styles strictly from `context.colorScheme` and `context.textTheme`.
   - Apply proper `ScreenUtil` responsive extensions on all paddings, margins, and sizes.

```dart
abstract class SplashEvent {
  const SplashEvent();
}

class SplashStarted extends SplashEvent {
  const SplashStarted();
}
```

lib/features/splash/present/bloc/splash_state.dart
```dart
abstract class SplashState {
  const SplashState();
}

class SplashInitial extends SplashState {
  const SplashInitial();
}

class SplashLoading extends SplashState {
  const SplashLoading();
}

class SplashSuccess extends SplashState {
  const SplashSuccess();
}

class SplashFailure extends SplashState {
  final String message;
  const SplashFailure(this.message);
}
```

lib/features/splash/present/bloc/splash_bloc.dart
```dart
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'splash_event.dart';
import 'splash_state.dart';

class SplashBloc extends Bloc<SplashEvent, SplashState> {
  SplashBloc() : super(const SplashInitial()) {
    on<SplashStarted>(_onSplashStarted);
  }

  Future<void> _onSplashStarted(
    SplashStarted event,
    Emitter<SplashState> emit,
  ) async {
    {
      emit(const SplashLoading());
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2));
      emit(const SplashSuccess());
    }
  }
}
```

lib/features/splash/present/views/splash_view.dart
```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:flutter_screenutil/flutter_screenutil.dart';
import '../bloc/splash_bloc.dart';
import '../bloc/splash_event.dart';
import '../bloc/splash_state.dart';

class SplashView extends StatelessWidget {
  const SplashView({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    {
      return BlocProvider(
        create: (context) {
          {
            return SplashBloc()..add(const SplashStarted());
          }
        },
        child: BlocListener<SplashBloc, SplashState>(
          listener: (context, state) {
            {
              if (state is SplashSuccess) {
                // TODO: Navigate to home
              }
            }
          },
          child: Scaffold(
            backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
            body: Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'App Splash',
                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineLarge?.copyWith(
                          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                        ),
                  ),
                  SizedBox(height: 24.h),
                  CircularProgressIndicator(
                    color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
  }
}
```

To refactor and improve the `lib/features/financial` feature according to the strict architecture, file length limit (max 100 lines), styling, and Flutter style guidelines, here is the direction for the editor engineer:

### 1. Structure & Directory Organization Check
Verify that `lib/features/financial` strictly follows Clean Architecture:
- `lib/features/financial/data/`: `models/`, `datasources/`, `repo_impl/`
- `lib/features/financial/domain/`: `entities/`, `repo/`, `usecases/`
- `lib/features/financial/presentation/` (or `present/`): `blocs/`, `views/` (screens, components, widgets)

### 2. File Length Refactoring (Max 100 lines)
- Review every file in `lib/features/financial` to ensure it does not exceed 100 lines.
- If a view or widget file is too large, split it into smaller isolated widgets under a `presentation/views/widgets/` subfolder.

### 3. Styling & Theme Rules
- **No Hardcoded Styles/Colors**: Ensure all colors use `context.colorScheme.xxx` or `Theme.of(context).colorScheme.xxx`.
- **Responsive Extensions**: Always use `.r`, `.w`, `.h`, `.sp` combined with dot shorthand where applicable (e.g., `padding: .all(16.r)`, `borderRadius: .circular(12.r)`).
- **Block Bodies**: Use full block bodies `{ return ...; }` for all methods, functions, and widget builders instead of arrow syntax (`=>`).
- **Icons**: Use `lucide_icons_flutter` or `font_awesome_flutter` instead of standard Material `Icons`.

### 4. Code Changes Example (Views / Widgets refactoring)
Ensure all widget builders inside `lib/features/financial/presentation/views/` look like this:

```dart
@override
Widget build(BuildContext context) {
  return Container(
    padding: .all(16.r),
    decoration: BoxDecoration(
      color: context.colorScheme.surface,
      borderRadius: .circular(12.r),
    ),
    child: Text(
      'Financial Summary',
      style: context.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
        color: context.colorScheme.primary,
      ),
    ),
  );
}
```

To complete the enhancement of the `lib/features/auth` feature according to the project's architecture, styling, file length limits, and component standards, follow these architect directions:

### 1. File Length & Modularization Audit (< 100 lines rule)
- Inspect all files under `lib/features/auth/` (including sub-features: `auth`, `change_pass`, `forgot_password`, `sign_in`, `sign_up`).
- Any file exceeding 100 lines must be split into smaller, cohesive stateless/stateful widgets or helper classes (e.g., separating form fields, buttons, and header sections into dedicated widgets under `presentation/views/widgets/`).

### 2. Clean Architecture Folder Structure
- Ensure strict separation of layers across all auth sub-modules:
  - `data/`: Models, data sources, repository implementations.
  - `domain/`: Entities, repository interfaces, use cases.
  - `presentation/`: BLoC/Cubit state management.
  - `present/views/`: UI screens and modularized widgets.

### 3. Styling & ScreenUtil Enforcement
- Remove all hardcoded colors (e.g., `Color(0xFF...)`, `Colors.green`) and text styles (`TextStyle(...)`).
- Replace them entirely with `Theme.of(context).colorScheme` and `Theme.of(context).textTheme`.
- Ensure all padding, margins, spacing, and border radii use ScreenUtil extensions (`.r`, `.w`, `.h`, `.sp`), e.g., `padding: .all(16.r)`, `borderRadius: .circular(12.r)`.
- Use block bodies `{ return ...; }` for all functions, methods, and widget builders (avoid arrow syntax `=>`).
- Use dot shorthand where applicable (e.g., `.center`, `.none`).

### 4. Components & Iconography Rules
- **Phone Fields:** Verify that any phone number input field uses `CustomPhoneFormField` from `@lib/core/widgets/forms/custom_phone_form_field.dart`.
- **Icons:** Replace any standard Material `Icons.xxx` with icons from `Fontawesome` or `lucide_icons_flutter` libraries.

To fully refactor and optimize the `lib/features/profile` feature according to the project's strict architecture, styling, and file-length rules, follow the directions below:

### 1. Directory Structure & Clean Architecture Check
Ensure `lib/features/profile/` strictly adheres to:
- `lib/features/profile/data/` (models, datasources, repo implementation)
- `lib/features/profile/domain/` (entities, repository abstract contracts, use cases)
- `lib/features/profile/present/` (bloc/cubit, state, event)
- `lib/features/profile/present/views/` (screens and modularized UI widgets)

### 2. File Length Limit Compliance (< 100 Lines)
- Check every file inside `lib/features/profile/` to ensure it does not exceed **100 lines of code**.
- If any view or widget file is too large, split it into smaller sub-widgets under `lib/features/profile/present/views/widgets/`.

### 3. Styling & Theming Refactor
- Remove any hardcoded colors (e.g., `Color(0xFF...)`, `Colors.white`) or explicit `TextStyle(...)` instantiations.
- Replace them with theme properties:
  - `Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary`
  - `Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium` etc.
- Ensure all paddings, margins, and borders use ScreenUtil extensions correctly with dot shorthand:
  - `padding: .all(16.r)`
  - `borderRadius: .circular(12.r)`
  - Avoid explicit `EdgeInsets.all` or `BorderRadius.circular` static calls.

### 4. Forms & Iconography
- For any phone number input fields, use `CustomPhoneFormField` from `@lib/core/widgets/forms/custom_phone_form_field.dart`.
- Replace any standard Material `Icons` with icons from `Fontawesome` or `lucide_icons_flutter`.

```dart
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

class ProfileEntity extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String email;
  final String phoneNumber;

  const ProfileEntity({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.email,
    required this.phoneNumber,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [id, name, email, phoneNumber];
}
```

```dart
import 'package:ai_agent_core/core/execute_guard/execute_guard.dart';
import '../entities/profile_entity.dart';

abstract class ProfileRepository {
  Future<ExecuteGuard<ProfileEntity>> getProfile();
  Future<ExecuteGuard<void>> updateProfile(ProfileEntity profile);
}
```

lib/features/profile/domain/usecases/get_profile_usecase.dart
```dart
import 'package:ai_agent_core/core/execute_guard/execute_guard.dart';
import '../entities/profile_entity.dart';
import '../repositories/profile_repository.dart';

class GetProfileUseCase {
  final ProfileRepository repository;

  const GetProfileUseCase(this.repository);

  Future<ExecuteGuard<ProfileEntity>> call() async {
    return await repository.getProfile();
  }
}
```

lib/features/profile/data/models/profile_model.dart
```dart
import '../../domain/entities/profile_entity.dart';

class ProfileModel extends ProfileEntity {
  const ProfileModel({
    required super.id,
    required super.name,
    required super.email,
    required super.phoneNumber,
  });

  factory ProfileModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return ProfileModel(
      id: json['id'] as String,
      name: json['name'] as String,
      email: json['email'] as String,
      phoneNumber: json['phone_number'] as String,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'email': email,
      'phone_number': phoneNumber,
    };
  }
}
```

lib/features/profile/data/repositories/profile_repository_impl.dart
```dart
import 'package:ai_agent_core/core/execute_guard/execute_guard.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/profile_entity.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/profile_repository.dart';
import '../datasources/profile_remote_datasource.dart';

class ProfileRepositoryImpl implements ProfileRepository {
  final ProfileRemoteDataSource remoteDataSource;

  const ProfileRepositoryImpl(this.remoteDataSource);

  @override
  Future<ExecuteGuard<ProfileEntity>> getProfile() async {
    return ExecuteGuard.guard(() async {
      return await remoteDataSource.getProfile();
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<ExecuteGuard<void>> updateProfile(ProfileEntity profile) async {
    return ExecuteGuard.guard(() async {
      await remoteDataSource.updateProfile(profile);
    });
  }
}
```

lib/features/profile/present/bloc/profile_state.dart
```dart
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/profile_entity.dart';

abstract class ProfileState extends Equatable {
  const ProfileState();

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class ProfileInitial extends ProfileState {}

class ProfileLoading extends ProfileState {}

class ProfileLoaded extends ProfileState {
  final ProfileEntity profile;

  const ProfileLoaded(this.profile);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [profile];
}

class ProfileError extends ProfileState {
  final String message;

  const ProfileError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}
```

lib/features/profile/present/bloc/profile_cubit.dart
```dart
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import '../../domain/usecases/get_profile_usecase.dart';
import 'profile_state.dart';

class ProfileCubit extends Cubit<ProfileState> {
  final GetProfileUseCase getProfileUseCase;

  ProfileCubit(this.getProfileUseCase) : super(ProfileInitial());

  Future<void> fetchProfile() async {
    emit(ProfileLoading());
    final result = await getProfileUseCase();
    result.when(
      success: (profile) => emit(ProfileLoaded(profile)),
      failure: (error) => emit(ProfileError(error.toString())),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:flutter_screenutil/flutter_screenutil.dart';
import 'package:lucide_icons_flutter/lucide_icons.dart';
import '../bloc/profile_cubit.dart';
import '../bloc/profile_state.dart';

class ProfileScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const ProfileScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(
          'Profile',
          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,
        ),
        leading: Icon(
          LucideIcons.user,
          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
        ),
      ),
      body: BlocBuilder<ProfileCubit, ProfileState>(
        builder: (context, state) {
          if (state is ProfileLoading) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          }
          if (state is ProfileLoaded) {
            return Padding(
              padding: .all(16.r),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: .start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    state.profile.name,
                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge,
                  ),
                  SizedBox(height: 8.h),
                  Text(
                    state.profile.email,
                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }
          if (state is ProfileError) {
            return Center(
              child: Text(
                state.message,
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
                    ),
              ),
            );
          }
          return const SizedBox.shrink();
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:formz/formz.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/profile_entity.dart';

part 'profile_state.freezed.dart';

@freezed
abstract class ProfileState with _$ProfileState {
  const factory ProfileState.loading() = _ProfileLoading;
  const factory ProfileState.loaded({
    required ProfileEntity profile,
    required FormzSubmissionStatus formzSubmissionStatus,
  }) = _Loaded;
  const factory ProfileState.success() = _Success;
  const factory ProfileState.failure({required String error}) = _Failure;
}
```

lib/features/notifications/present/bloc/notifications_bloc.dart
```dart
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import '../../domain/usecases/get_notifications_usecase.dart';
import 'notifications_state.dart';

class NotificationsBloc extends Cubit<NotificationsState> {
  final GetNotificationsUseCase _getNotificationsUseCase;

  NotificationsBloc(this._getNotificationsUseCase) : super(const NotificationsInitial());

  Future<void> fetchNotifications() async {
    emit(const NotificationsLoading());
    final result = await _getNotificationsUseCase();
    result.fold(
      (exception) {
        emit(NotificationsError(exception.toString()));
      },
      (notifications) {
        emit(NotificationsLoaded(notifications));
      },
    );
  }
}
```

lib/features/notifications/present/bloc/notifications_state.dart
```dart
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/notification_entity.dart';

abstract class NotificationsState extends Equatable {
  const NotificationsState();

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class NotificationsInitial extends NotificationsState {
  const NotificationsInitial();
}

class NotificationsLoading extends NotificationsState {
  const NotificationsLoading();
}

class NotificationsLoaded extends NotificationsState {
  final List<NotificationEntity> notifications;

  const NotificationsLoaded(this.notifications);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [notifications];
}

class NotificationsError extends NotificationsState {
  final String message;

  const NotificationsError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}
```

lib/features/notifications/present/views/notifications_view.dart
```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:flutter_screenutil/flutter_screenutil.dart';
import '../bloc/notifications_bloc.dart';
import '../bloc/notifications_state.dart';
import '../widgets/notification_item_widget.dart';

class NotificationsView extends StatelessWidget {
  const NotificationsView({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(
          'Notifications',
          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,
        ),
      ),
      body: BlocBuilder<NotificationsBloc, NotificationsState>(
        builder: (context, state) {
          if (state is NotificationsLoading) {
            return Center(
              child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
              ),
            );
          }
          if (state is NotificationsLoaded) {
            if (state.notifications.isEmpty) {
              return Center(
                child: Text(
                  'No notifications found',
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
                ),
              );
            }
            return ListView.separated(
              padding: EdgeInsets.all(16.r),
              itemCount: state.notifications.length,
              separatorBuilder: (context, index) {
                return SizedBox(height: 12.h);
              },
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                return NotificationItemWidget(
                  notification: state.notifications[index],
                );
              },
            );
          }
          if (state is NotificationsError) {
            return Center(
              child: Text(
                state.message,
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                  color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
                ),
              ),
            );
          }
          return const SizedBox.shrink();
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class NotificationItemWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final NotificationEntity notification;

  const NotificationItemWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.notification,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(16.r),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12.r),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.shadow.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 8.r,
            offset: Offset(0, 2.h),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            notification.title,
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 8.h),
          Text(
            notification.body,
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

To complete this task and refactor the `lib/features/financial` feature according to Clean Architecture, file size limits (< 100 lines), theme-driven styling with ScreenUtil, and rule constraints, here are the instructions for the editor engineer.

### 1. Folder Structure Check & Modularization
Ensure `lib/features/financial/` follows this layout:
```text
lib/features/financial/
├── data/
│   ├── datasources/     # (API / local data sources)
│   ├── models/          # (Request/Response models)
│   └── repo_impl/       # (Repository implementations)
├── domain/
│   ├── entities/        # (Entities with modification checking if needed)
│   ├── repo/            # (Abstract repository contracts)
│   └── usecases/        # (Business use cases)
└── present/
    ├── blocs/           # (Bloc / Cubit files split into event, state, bloc)
    └── views/           # (UI screens and sub-widgets, each under 100 lines)
```

---

### 2. File Length & Component Refactoring Rules
- **Rule:** No single file in `lib/features/financial/` may exceed **100 lines of code**.
- **Action:** If any view or bloc file exceeds 100 lines, split it into smaller widget components under a `widgets/` folder inside `present/`.

---

### 3. Styling & Flutter Style Standards
- **ZERO HARDCODED STYLES & COLORS:**
  - Never use explicit colors like `Color(0xFF...)` or `Colors.white`.
  - Always use `Theme.of(context).colorScheme` and `Theme.of(context).textTheme`.
- **ScreenUtil Extensions:**
  - Always use `.r`, `.w`, `.h`, `.sp` with dot shorthand (e.g., `.all(16.r)`, `.symmetric(horizontal: 16.w, vertical: 12.h)`, `borderRadius: .circular(12.r)`).
  - Never use explicit `EdgeInsets.all()` or `BorderRadius.circular()` without extensions.
- **Block Bodies:**
  - Use full block bodies `{ return ...; }` for methods and widget builders instead of arrow syntax (`=>`).

---

### 4. Iconography & Form Rules
- Use icons **strictly** from `Fontawesome` or `lucide_icons_flutter` (avoid standard `Icons.xxx`).
- For any phone number input fields, use `CustomPhoneFormField` at `@lib/core/widgets/forms/custom_phone_form_field.dart`.

### 1. Clean Architecture Directory Structure
Ensure the feature strictly follows the separation of concerns:
- `lib/features/orders_features/data/`

### 2. File Length Limit (Max 100 Lines)

- Proactively split large UI files into smaller, focused widgets under a `widgets/` subfolder within `present/`.

### 3. Theme-Driven Styling & ScreenUtil

- **ALWAYS** use ScreenUtil extensions for padding, margins, and borders (e.g., `16.r`, `12.h`, `8.w`, `12.r` for `BorderRadius.circular`).

```dart
class OrderEntity {
  final String id;
  final String title;
  final double amount;

  const OrderEntity({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.amount,
  });

  bool hasChanges(OrderEntity original) {
    {
      return title != original.title || amount != original.amount;
    }
  }
}
```

```dart
}
```

```dart
import 'package:core/core.dart';
import '../entities/order_entity.dart';
import '../repositories/orders_repository.dart';

class GetOrdersUseCase {
  final OrdersRepository repository;

  const GetOrdersUseCase({required this.repository});

  Future<ExecuteGuard<List<OrderEntity>>> call() {
    {
      return repository.getOrders();
    }
  }
}
```

lib/features/orders_features/present/bloc/orders_cubit.dart
```dart
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import '../../domain/usecases/get_orders_usecase.dart';
import 'orders_state.dart';

class OrdersCubit extends Cubit<OrdersState> {
  final GetOrdersUseCase getOrdersUseCase;

  OrdersCubit({required this.getOrdersUseCase}) : super(const OrdersInitial());

  Future<void> fetchOrders() async {
    {
      emit(const OrdersLoading());
      final result = await getOrdersUseCase();
      result.fold(
        (failure) {
          emit(OrdersError(message: failure.message));
        },
        (orders) {
          emit(OrdersLoaded(orders: orders));
        },
      );
    }
  }
}
```

lib/features/orders_features/present/bloc/orders_state.dart
```dart
import '../../domain/entities/order_entity.dart';

abstract class OrdersState {
  const OrdersState();
}

class OrdersInitial extends OrdersState {
  const OrdersInitial();
}

class OrdersLoading extends OrdersState {
  const OrdersLoading();
}

class OrdersLoaded extends OrdersState {
  final List<OrderEntity> orders;
  const OrdersLoaded({required this.orders});
}

class OrdersError extends OrdersState {
  final String message;
  const OrdersError({required this.message});
}
```

lib/features/orders_features/present/views/orders_view.dart
```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:flutter_screenutil/flutter_screenutil.dart';
import '../../domain/usecases/get_orders_usecase.dart';
import '../bloc/orders_cubit.dart';
import '../bloc/orders_state.dart';

class OrdersView extends StatelessWidget {
  const OrdersView({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    {
      return Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(
          title: Text(
            'Orders',
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,
          ),
          backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
        ),
        body: Padding(
          padding: .all(16.r),
          child: BlocBuilder<OrdersCubit, OrdersState>(
            builder: (context, state) {
              {
                if (state is OrdersLoading) {
                  {
                    return Center(
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                      ),
                    );
                  }
                }
                if (state is OrdersLoaded) {
                  {
                    return ListView.builder(
                      itemCount: state.orders.length,
                      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                        {
                          final order = state.orders[index];
                          return Card(
                            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
                            margin: .only(bottom: 12.h),
                            child: Padding(
                              padding: .all(16.r),
                              child: Text(
                                order.title,
                                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        }
                      },
                    );
                  }
                }
                if (state is OrdersError) {
                  {
                    return Center(
                      child: Text(
                        state.message,
                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
                            ),
                      ),
                    );
                  }
                }
                return const SizedBox.shrink();
              }
            },
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
  }
}
```

To refactor and align the `lib/features/auth` feature with the project's architecture and coding standards, follow these engineering directives:

### 1. File Length & Modularization (100-Line Limit Rule)
- Inspect all files under `lib/features/auth/` (including sub-features: `sign_in`, `sign_up`, `forgot_password`, `change_pass`).
- If any file (especially UI views or BLoCs) exceeds **100 lines of code**, split it immediately into smaller, cohesive sub-widgets or helper classes.
- Ensure proper barrel files or direct relative imports are maintained.

### 2. Zero Hardcoded Styles & Theme-Driven Styling
- Audit all presentation widgets and screens inside `lib/features/auth` for explicit colors (`Color(0xFF...)`, `Colors.xxx`) or hardcoded `TextStyle`.
- Replace them entirely with:
  - Colors: `Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary`, `surface`, etc.
  - Typography: `Theme.of(context).textTheme...`
- **ScreenUtil & Dot Shorthand Integration**: Ensure all paddings, margins, and border radii use dot shorthand with responsive extensions:
  - `padding: .all(16.r)`
  - `borderRadius: .circular(12.r)`
  - Avoid any explicit `EdgeInsets.` or `BorderRadius.` static calls.

### 3. Components & Iconography Rules
- **Phone Fields**: In any sign-up or profile-related forms within `lib/features/auth`, replace standard text form fields used for phone numbers with `CustomPhoneFormField` located at `lib/core/widgets/forms/custom_phone_form_field.dart`.
- **Icons**: Replace any standard Material `Icons.xxx` with icons from `Fontawesome` or `lucide_icons_flutter`.

### 4. Clean Architecture Verification
- Verify that every sub-module under `lib/features/auth/` strictly adheres to:
  - `data/`: Models, datasources, repository implementations.
  - `domain/`: Entities, abstract repository interfaces, use cases.
  - `present/`: BLoCs/Cubits and view files under `present/views/`.

```
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_screenutil/flutter_screenutil.dart';
import '../../../../../../core/di/dependency_injection.dart';
import '../../../../../../core/theme/core/extensions/theme_ext.dart';
import '../../../../cached_docs/data/datasources/local_data_src/drift/tables/items_table.dart';
import '../../../../cached_docs/data/datasources/local_data_src/drift/file_upload_status_extension.dart';
import '../../bloc/order_items_bloc.dart';

class OrdersFilterWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  const OrdersFilterWidget({super.key, required this.selectedStatus});
  final UploadStatus? selectedStatus;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final hasFilter = selectedStatus != null;
    return Row(
      mainAxisSize: .min,
      children: [
        if (hasFilter)
          AnimatedSwitcher(
            duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
            transitionBuilder: (child, animation) {
              return ScaleTransition(scale: animation, child: child);
            },
            child: IconButton(
              tooltip: 'إلغاء الفلتر',
              icon: Icon(Icons.close, size: 28.r),
              color: context.colorScheme.inverseSurface,
              style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                foregroundColor: context.colorScheme.error,
                backgroundColor: context.colorScheme.errorContainer.withValues(
                  alpha: 0.2,
                ),
                padding: .all(8.r),
              ),
              onPressed: () {
                getIt<OrderItemsBloc>().add(const .filterChanged(status: null));
              },
            ),
          ),
        PopupMenuButton<UploadStatus?>(
          tooltip: 'فلترة الطلبات',
          elevation: 50,
          borderRadius: .circular(12.r),
          color: context.colorScheme.surface,
          surfaceTintColor: context.colorScheme.surfaceTint,
          shadowColor: context.colorScheme.shadow.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
          position: .over,
          icon: AnimatedContainer(
            duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
            padding: .all(2.r),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: hasFilter
                  ? selectedStatus!.color.withValues(alpha: 0.12)
                  : context.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest.withValues(
                      alpha: 0.5,
                    ),
              borderRadius: .circular(12.r),
              border: .all(
                color: hasFilter
                    ? selectedStatus!.color.withValues(alpha: 0.5)
                    : Colors.transparent,
                width: 1.5,
              ),
            ),
            child: Icon(
              hasFilter ? Icons.filter_alt : Icons.filter_alt_outlined,
              size: 28.r,
              color: hasFilter
                  ? selectedStatus!.color
                  : context.colorScheme.inverseSurface,
            ),
          ),
          onSelected: (status) {
            getIt<OrderItemsBloc>().add(.filterChanged(status: status));
          },
          itemBuilder: (context) {
            return [
              PopupMenuItem<UploadStatus?>(
                value: null,
                height: 44.h,
                onTap: () {
                  getIt<OrderItemsBloc>().add(
                    const .filterChanged(status: null),
                  );
                },
                child: Container(
                  padding: .symmetric(horizontal: 2.w, vertical: 2.h),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: selectedStatus == null
                        ? context.colorScheme.primaryContainer.withValues(
                            alpha: 0.2,
                          )
                        : Colors.transparent,
                    borderRadius: .circular(8.r),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(
                        selectedStatus == null
                            ? Icons.radio_button_checked_rounded
                            : Icons.radio_button_off_rounded,
                        color: selectedStatus == null
                            ? context.colorScheme.primary
                            : context.colorScheme.outline,
                        size: 20.r,
                      ),
                      SizedBox(width: 12.w),
                      Text(
                        'كل الطلبات',
                        style: context.textTheme.labelMedium?.copyWith(
                          fontWeight: selectedStatus == null ? .bold : .normal,
                          color: selectedStatus == null
                              ? context.colorScheme.primary
                              : context.colorScheme.onSurface,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const PopupMenuDivider(),
              ...UploadStatus.values.map((status) {
                final isSelected = selectedStatus == status;
                return PopupMenuItem<UploadStatus?>(
                  value: status,
                  height: 44.h,
                  child: Container(
                    padding: .symmetric(vertical: 4.h, horizontal: 4.w),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: isSelected
                          ? status.color.withValues(alpha: 0.12)
                          : Colors.transparent,
                      borderRadius: .circular(8.r),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Icon(
                          isSelected
                              ? Icons.check_circle_rounded
                              : Icons.circle_outlined,
                          color: isSelected
                              ? status.color
                              : context.colorScheme.outline,
                          size: 20.r,
                        ),
                        SizedBox(width: 10.w),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            status.label,
                            style: context.textTheme.labelMedium?.copyWith(
                              fontWeight: isSelected ? .bold : .normal,
                              color: isSelected
                                  ? status.color
                                  : context.colorScheme.onSurface,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        SizedBox(width: 8.w),
                        Icon(
                          status.icon,
                          color: isSelected
                              ? status.color
                              : status.color.withValues(alpha: 0.6),
                          size: 18.r,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              }),
            ];
          },
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```
